## Assets embedded by the optional PDF writer

Krilla 0.8.2 bundles the following Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram, under CC0-1.0.
The immutable source revision is `3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5`, recorded by the
registry package's `.cargo_vcs_info.json`. These are the same dependency profiles already
used by the apps' Krilla exporters. The local backend snapshot retains these four profiles; the optional writer adds no font binaries.
The profile licence is retained in `licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt`.

| Crate / file | Author | Source | Licence | SHA-256 | Use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sGrey-v2-magic.icc` | Clinton Ingram | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sGrey-v2-magic.icc | CC0-1.0 | `4110169287e5f1b5149ca9f1da1814d32943905bba8030e849d1a3b729a19481` | PDF writer colour profiles |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sGrey-v4.icc` | Clinton Ingram | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sGrey-v4.icc | CC0-1.0 | `00c0f94e09127520a17dc0e1d9264b5702081d96dbdb1549ee88e3631ce42a9d` | PDF writer colour profiles |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sRGB-v2-magic.icc` | Clinton Ingram | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sRGB-v2-magic.icc | CC0-1.0 | `af4efe28f6d311799865f325ba39184a2e978b113ca74124d60af7de22b105f4` | PDF writer colour profiles |

The four Krilla ICC entries above are also stored unchanged in `vendor/krilla/icc`.
The vendored source patches are in `vendor/krilla/patches`, described in `vendor/krilla/README.craft.md`; per-file hashes are in `vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json`.

## Assets retained with the optional PDF reader

The shared reader centralizes the five patched Hayro source trees from PdfCraft and the
patched crates.io `hayro-postscript` 0.1.0 scanner, which bundles no assets.
The standard-14 fonts, CMYK profile and generated metrics listed below remain byte-identical
in `vendor/hayro-interpret`, and the CMaps in `vendor/hayro-cmap/assets`; no application
document fonts are added. The removed upstream LAB.icc profile is not restored. Source hashes
and the exact transfer revision are recorded in each vendored crate's SOURCE.json.

PREAMBLE
The goals of the Open Font License (OFL) are to stimulate worldwide
development of collaborative font projects, to support the font creation
efforts of academic and linguistic communities, and to provide a free and
open framework in which fonts may be shared and improved in partnership
with others.

redistributed freely as long as they are not sold by themselves. The
fonts, including any derivative works, can be bundled, embedded, 
redistributed and/or sold with any software provided that any reserved
names are not used by derivative works. The fonts and derivatives,
however, cannot be released under any other type of license. The
requirement for fonts to remain under this license does not apply
to any document created using the fonts or their derivatives.

copyright statement(s).

distributed by the Copyright Holder(s).

or substituting -- in part or in whole -- any of the components of the

new environment.

  Source: Fogra (Fogra Forschungsinstitut für Medientechnologien e.V.), www.fogra.org, from
  https://fogra.org/fileadmin/files/7_downloads/Charakterisierungsdaten/FOGRA39_to_FOGRA60_v2.zip
  Fogra's terms: "Free use and redistribution of the Fogra characterisation data is permitted,

  the data are distributed unmodified and Fogra is identified as the source. The designation
  FOGRAxx may be used solely to identify the respective reference data. Such use does not
  imply certification, approval or endorsement by Fogra."

  Grady Ward (Project Gutenberg eBook #3204).
crates/text/assets/hyphen/en-us.pat: CC0-1.0, trained from en-us.dic by DesignCraft's trainer.
Hyphenation patterns for other languages, embedded through the hypher crate only when the
  matching craft-text feature is enabled (none are by default). They come from hyph-utf8,
  https://github.com/hyphenation/tex-hyphen, as packaged in https://github.com/typst/hypher
  (patterns/). Each file is under the license stated in its header: LPPL, MIT, BSD-3-Clause, the
  Unlicense, a notice permitting any use, or a choice of licenses that includes one of these. An
  application that enables a language ships that file's copyright and license notice. The
  exception is Slovak (hyph-sk.tex, Copyright (C) 1992 Jana Chlebíková), under the GNU General
  Public License, version 2 or later (SPDX: GPL-2.0-or-later). craft-text asks for it through the
  feature "slovak-gpl", which "all-languages" does not include; an application that enables it
  ships GPL-licensed data. Cargo unifies features, so another dependency that enables hypher's
  "slovak" feature (hypher's default features do) embeds the same data; a build that must exclude
  it checks its dependency graph. The Spanish patterns (feature "spanish") are hyph-es.tex 5.0.
  Copyright (C) 1993, 1997 Javier Bezos, 2001-2019 Javier Bezos, CervanTeX. MIT License:
  Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software
  and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without
  restriction, including without limitation the rights to use, copy, modify, merge, publish,
  distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the
  Software is furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions: The above copyright notice
  and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of the
  Software. THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED,
  INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY, FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE
  AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM,
  DAMAGES OR OTHER LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
  OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE SOFTWARE.
- Word-break dictionaries and LSTM models for Thai, Lao, Khmer and Burmese, embedded through the
  icu_segmenter_data crate only when craft-text's "thai", "lao", "khmer" or "burmese" feature is

and vendor/krilla/SOURCE.json. Its upstream MIT/Apache-2.0 licences and CC0 profiles are unchanged.

Optional PDF reader

Hayro 0.7.1, hayro-interpret 0.7.0, hayro-syntax 0.7.2, hayro-jbig2 0.3.0, hayro-cmap 0.1.0
and hayro-postscript 0.1.0 (https://github.com/LaurenzV/hayro), MIT OR Apache-2.0, retain their
upstream licence files. The first five are patched sources transferred from PdfCraft
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft): hayro-cmap from revision
a2ec27b4775f53c40cad3456936d9406611e3a8d, the others from revision
ec0a965074f376a5bf610435948e4e5184bf3299. hayro-postscript is the crates.io 0.1.0 package
(upstream revision a2354aebaf86f5ab13e5fd6b6b0521306488bf9d) with one local patch.
See vendor/README.craft-reader.md and each crate's SOURCE.json for provenance and local changes.
The hayro-interpret plate mode (separations, overprint and output-intent simulation) is ported
from PdfCraft pull request #2770 by Guillem Hermida, commit
6fcf2a4aca29e943e28cb69017ff8f87d00656c6 (https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2770),
MIT OR Apache-2.0; crates/pdf/tests/plates.rs and tests/support/synthetic_icc.rs adapt its tests.
Knockout transparency groups in hayro-interpret and hayro are ported from PdfCraft pull request
#2974 by mtlcl410, commit bae69819594a6385a027fec1f67e6b3c36c259b4
(https://github.com/storytold/pdfcraft/pull/2974), MIT OR Apache-2.0;
crates/pdf/tests/knockout.rs adapts its test.
The reader retains the existing Foxit/PDFium standard-14 font assets (BSD-3-Clause),
Compact ICC Profiles by Clinton Ingram (CC0-1.0), and dependency CMaps (BSD-3-Clause).
Their licences and per-file hashes are retained in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets,
vendor/hayro-cmap/assets, licenses/pdf-backend and ATTRIBUTION.md. These are existing reader dependency assets;
applications retain their own document-font selection and licensing policy.

The Canon CRW decoder in crates/crw (src/lib.rs, src/tables.rs, src/tests.rs, tests/) and its
derivation record (crates/crw/clean-room/) come from:
- LightCraft, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw.rs, crates/raw/src/vendor/crw_tables.rs,
  crates/raw/tests/crw_oracle.rs, crates/raw/tests/it/vendor_robust.rs and clean-room/crw/,
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft/pull/3021, revision 6ca439b5155820d2befeacd81ec0c6a80b2bd3bc
  (decoder ported at 70c755e42a65661401142819278d51b0c5977f7f; the record is identical at both).
  https://github.com/storytold/lightcraft
  Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the LightCraft contributors.
Its white-level plateau estimate (saturation_plateau) is LightCraft's vendor::white_from_data at the
same revision. The CRW compression was derived clean-room from CC0 sample files and the
maintainer's own photographs by black-box comparison with a reference decoder's command-line
output; no raw-decoder source was consulted (crates/crw/clean-room/sources.md).

      "control" means (i) the power, direct or indirect, to cause the

      otherwise, or (ii) ownership of fifty percent (50%) or more of the
      outstanding shares, or (iii) beneficial ownership of such entity.

      not limited to compiled object code, generated documentation,
      and conversions to other media types.

      (an example is provided in the Appendix below).

      "Contributor" shall mean Licensor and any individual or Legal Entity
      on behalf of whom a Contribution has been received by Licensor and

===== texts/d25d9a2fc5a9f35ccf6819541e04110f6329e5640806e08a25ce2ffbb14d6cae.txt =====
# Attribution

Every non-code asset in EffectCraft, with its author, source and licence. Each repository file listed here also has
a `<file>.attribution` sidecar with full details. The rules are in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) §1: no Adobe
iconography, images or other Adobe assets; open licences only; every asset attributed.
`cargo xtask assets` checks that this index and the sidecars cover every asset in the repository.
The complete licence texts and copyright notices for the bundled fonts, including the fonts embedded
by the pinned egui dependency, are reproduced in [`NOTICE`](NOTICE), which every package ships; the
`assets/fonts/OFL-*.txt` files are the upstream copies kept beside the fonts.

## Bundled dependency fonts

These fonts are embedded by the pinned egui dependency (epaint_default_fonts 0.37 at
`6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a`); no font file is added to this repository.
egui-icons is always embedded, `default_fonts` adds Ubuntu Light and Hack, and
`monochrome_emoji_fonts` adds Noto Emoji and emoji-icon-font. The complete copyright notices
and licence texts are in [`NOTICE`](NOTICE).

| Asset | Author | Source | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| `epaint_default_fonts/fonts/egui-icons.ttf` | John Slegers (emoji-icon-font) | https://github.com/storytold/egui/blob/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts/egui-icons.txt; original https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font | MIT (complete copyright and permission text in `NOTICE`) |
| `epaint_default_fonts/fonts/emoji-icon-font.ttf` | John Slegers | https://github.com/storytold/egui/blob/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts/emoji-icon-font-mit-license.txt; original https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font | MIT (complete copyright and permission text in `NOTICE`) |
| `epaint_default_fonts/fonts/NotoEmoji-Regular.ttf` | Google Inc. (The Noto Project Authors) | https://github.com/storytold/egui/blob/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts/OFL.txt | OFL-1.1 (complete text in `NOTICE`) |
| `epaint_default_fonts/fonts/Ubuntu-Light.ttf` | Canonical Ltd. and Dalton Maag | https://github.com/storytold/egui/blob/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts/UFL.txt | Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0 (complete text in `NOTICE`) |

One small After Effects test project of `crates/aep` is in this repository: `crates/aep/tests/fixtures/synthetic.aep` (original work, made with After Effects 2026 from own data, working space None, the embedded colour profile removed; MIT OR Apache-2.0, with its `.attribution` sidecar). The other After Effects test projects of `crates/aep` are not in this repository. They are original work of an EffectCraft contributor, generated with After Effects 2026 as own test data (MIT OR Apache-2.0, each file with its `.attribution` sidecar); they are in [storytold/effectcraft-corpus](https://github.com/storytold/effectcraft-corpus) with their sidecars, fetched by `cargo xtask aep-corpus` (commit-pinned, SHA-256-verified), and the tests read them only from the folder named by `EFFECTCRAFT_AEP_FIXTURES` (opt-in). Their embedded colour profiles are replaced by stubs made with `crates/aep/examples/stubicc.rs` (a header and a description, no colour data), so no vendor profile data is in them.

## Code-drawn assets (original work, MIT OR Apache-2.0)

- UI icons drawn in code: `crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs`, including the original filled favorite star (2026-10-11, EffectCraft contributors).
- Procedural demo content (solids, shapes, gradients, text animations): `crates/engine/src/demo.rs`.
- Built-in project templates (Lower Third, Title Card, Logo Reveal, Kinetic Type, Social Square, Vertical Story,
  Slideshow, 3D Text Orbit) built from engine commands, and their thumbnails rendered by our own renderer:
  `crates/engine/src/templates.rs`.
- Text animator presets (Typewriter, Fade Up Characters, Bounce In Words, Tracking In, Scramble, Blur In, Jitter,
  Drop In Lines): `crates/engine/presets/text_animators.json` (sidecar `.attribution`).
- Transition animation presets (23: wipes, clock and fan reveals, blinds, dissolves, card flips, CC wipes, fades):
  `crates/engine/presets/transitions.json` (sidecar `.attribution`).
- Backgrounds animation presets (23: drifting gradients, fog, cloud and smoke, plasma and magma, cells and crystals, scrolling grids
  and checks, pulse rings, bokeh, light sweep): `crates/engine/presets/backgrounds.json` (sidecar `.attribution`).
- Behaviors animation presets (22: wiggle, shake, float, orbit, bounce, drift, pendulum, spin, pulse, heartbeat, blink, strobe, flicker):
  `crates/engine/presets/behaviors.json` (sidecar `.attribution`).
- Sample scripts (File ▸ Scripts) and ScriptUI panel: `crates/engine/scripts/Rename Layers.jsx`,
  `crates/engine/scripts/Sort Layers by In Point.jsx`, `crates/engine/scripts/Create Null at Selected Layers.jsx`,
  `crates/engine/scripts/Render Queue Batch.jsx`, `crates/engine/scripts/ScriptUI Panels/Layer Tools.jsx`
  (sidecars `.attribution`).
- Bundled extension (ScriptUI panel) with its twelve built-in ease presets:
  `extensions/scriptui-panels/Ease Presets.jsx` (sidecar `.attribution`).
- Example effect plug-in: `examples/plugins/posterize-bands` (plug-in API v1, WebAssembly).

## Trained models (optional downloads, not bundled)

The repository and the installers contain no model weights. These models are downloaded only when
a user asks (Settings ▸ Roto Brush, Settings ▸ Face Tracking, or the `roto.model.*` /
`face.model.*` commands), unmodified from their authors' official location, checked against a
pinned SHA-256 and stored in the user's `models` folder with a `<file>.NOTICE.txt` naming their
authors, licence and source. EffectCraft runs them with its own Rust implementation and is not
affiliated with or endorsed by their authors.

| Model | Authors | Source | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| MobileSAM (`mobile_sam.pt`) | Chaoning Zhang et al., building on Meta AI's Segment Anything and Microsoft's TinyViT | https://github.com/ChaoningZhang/MobileSAM | Apache-2.0 |
| MediaPipe Face Landmarker (`face_landmarker.task`: BlazeFace short-range detector, Face Mesh V2, blendshapes) | Google LLC | https://developers.google.com/edge/mediapipe/solutions/vision/face_landmarker (model cards: https://storage.googleapis.com/mediapipe-assets/MediaPipe%20BlazeFace%20Model%20Card%20(Short%20Range).pdf, https://storage.googleapis.com/mediapipe-assets/Model%20Card%20MediaPipe%20Face%20Mesh%20V2.pdf) | Apache-2.0 |

## Formats and parameters from other open-source projects

Written in Rust for EffectCraft; no code is copied. They follow published specifications and
configuration from these Apache-2.0 projects:

- `crates/segment/src/tflite.rs` reads TensorFlow Lite files using the field layout of TensorFlow
  Lite's `schema.fbs` (The TensorFlow Authors, https://github.com/tensorflow/tensorflow,
  Apache-2.0).
- `crates/segment/src/mediapipe.rs` follows MediaPipe's published face landmarker graph: the SSD
  anchor layout, detection decoding, crop sizes and the face-mesh point indices it uses (Google
  LLC, https://github.com/google-ai-edge/mediapipe, Apache-2.0).
- `crates/segment/src/mobilesam.rs` follows the MobileSAM, TinyViT and Segment Anything
  architectures and their Apache-2.0 reference code.

EffectCraft
Copyright (c) 2026 ArtCraft Team and the EffectCraft contributors.
Licensed under the MIT License or the Apache License, Version 2.0, at your option
(LICENSE-MIT, LICENSE-APACHE).

The ArtCraft name, wordmark and logos in docs/brand/ are trademarks of the ArtCraft Team. They are
not open source and are not covered by the license above; their terms are in
docs/brand/LICENSE-brand.txt.

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

Bundled fonts

EffectCraft embeds Inter, Noto Serif, JetBrains Mono and Noto Sans Symbols 2 from
assets/fonts/. The pinned egui dependency (epaint_default_fonts, revision
6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a) additionally embeds Ubuntu Light, Hack,
Noto Emoji, egui-icons and emoji-icon-font through EffectCraft's enabled `default_fonts`
and `monochrome_emoji_fonts` features; no font file from that dependency is added to
this repository. The complete copyright notices and licence texts required for all of
these fonts follow.

The optional PDF document reader uses craft-pdf and Hayro-syntax 0.7.2, MIT OR Apache-2.0.
The shared patched syntax source is centralized in storytold/craft-libs, transferred from
PdfCraft revision 9ed3abe4907a127d36b12fbfeee71d7e26248da1. This parser-only use embeds no
Hayro fonts, CMaps or image codecs; EffectCraft keeps its existing fonts and drawing filters.

Linux Wayland window decorations
-------------------------------
sctk-adwaita 0.10.1 (https://github.com/PolyMeilex/sctk-adwaita,
commit d5f343c45c04d4a026db57f76760819b7325db00) is used by winit on Linux
for vector window controls. Its title-font features are disabled: no Cantarell
font is embedded by this dependency and no font file is added to this repo.
